records.slice(0, 6);\n    case 'second-half':\n      return records.slice(6, 12);\n    case 'all':\n    default:\n      return [...records];\n  }\n};\n\nexport const calculateKpiSummary = (records: MonthlyRecord[] = []): KpiCardData[] => {\n  if (records.length === 0) {\n    return [\n      {\n        title: '売上高',\n        value: '0',\n        subValue: '目標達成率 0.0%',\n        changeRate: 0,\n        isPositive: true,\n        unit: '円',\n        type: 'sales',\n      },\n      {\n        title: '客数',\n        value: '0',\n        subValue: '月平均 0人',\n        changeRate: 0,\n isPositive: true,\n        unit: '円',\n        type: 'averageSpend',\n      },\n    ];\n  }\n\n  const totalSales = records.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.sales, 0);\n  const totalTargetSales = records.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.targetSales, 0);\n  const totalCustomers = records.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.customers, 0);\n  const avgMonthlyCustomers = Math.round(totalCustomers / records.length);\n  const overallAvgSpend = totalCustomers > 0 ? Math.round(totalSales / totalCustomers) : 0;\n  const maxSpend = Math.max(...records.map((r) => r.averageSpend));\n\n  const targetAchievementRate =\n    totalTargetSales > 0 ? ((totalSales / totalTargetSales) * 100).toFixed(1) : '0.0';\n\n  const lastIndex = records.length - 1;\n  const currentRecord = records[lastIndex];\n  const prevRecord = lastIndex > 0 ? records[lastIndex - 1] : records[0];\n\n  const salesChangeRate =\n    prevRecord.sales > 0\n      ? Number((((currentRecord.sales - prevRecord.sales) / prevRecord.sales) * 100).toFixed(1))\n      : 0;\n\n  const customersChangeRate =\n    prevRecord.customers > 0\n      ? Number((((currentRecord.customers - prevRecord.customers) / prevRecord.customers) * 100).toFixed(1))\n      : 0;\n\n  const spendChangeRate =\n    prevRecord.averageSpend > 0\n      ? Number((((currentRecord.averageSpend - prevRecord.averageSpend) / prevRecord.averageSpend) * 100).toFixed(1))\n      : 0;\n\n  return [\n    {\n      title: '売上高',\n      value: formatNumber(totalSales),\n      subValue: `目標達成率 ${targetAchievementRate}%`,\n      changeRate: salesChangeRate,\n      isPositive: salesChangeRate >= 0,\n      unit: '円',\n      type: 'sales',\n    },\n    {\n      title: '客数',\n      value: formatNumber(totalCustomers),\n      subValue: `月平均 ${formatNumber(avgMonthlyCustomers)}人`,\n      changeRate: customersChangeRate,\n      isPositive: customersChangeRate >= 0,\n      unit: '人',\n      type: 'customers',\n    },\n    {\n      title: '平均単価',\n      value: formatNumber(overallAvgSpend),\n      subValue: `最高単価 ${formatNumber(maxSpend)}円`,\n      changeRate: spendChangeRate,\n      isPositive: spendChangeRate >= 0,\n      unit: '円',\n      type: 'averageSpend',\n    },\n  ];\n};"},{"path":"src/App.tsx","description":"monthlyRecordsの安全な受け取りとイテレーション処理・フィルタリングロジックの修正","dependencies":["src/mockData.ts","src/types.ts","src/components/KpiCards.tsx","src/components/ChartsView.tsx"],"action":"modify","existingCode":"import maximumFractionDigits: 0,\n  }).format(val);\n};\n\nexport const formatNumber = (val: number): string => {\n  return new Intl.NumberFormat('ja-JP').format(Math.round(val));\n};\n\nexport const 'averageSpend',\n      },\n    ];\n  }\n\n  const totalSales = safeRecords.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (item.sales || 0), 0);\n  const totalTargetSales = safeRecords.reduce((sum, item) => sum + safeRecords.length);\n  const overallAvgSpend = totalCustomers > 0 ? Math.round(totalSales / totalCustomers) : 0;\n  const maxSpend = Math.max(...safeRecords.map((r) => r.averageSpend || 0));\n\n  const targetAchievementRate =\n    totalTargetSales > 0 ? Number((((currentRecord.sales - prevRecord.sales) / prevRecord.sales) * 100).toFixed(1))\n      : 0;\n\n  const customersChangeRate =\n    prevRecord && prevRecord.customers > 0\n      ? Number((((currentRecord.customers - prevRecord.customers) / prevRecord.customers) * 100).toFixed(1))\n      : 0;\n\n  const spendChangeRate =\n    prevRecord && prevRecord.averageSpend > 0\n      ? Number((((currentRecord.averageSpend - prevRecord.averageSpend) / prevRecord.averageSpend) * 100).toFixed(1))\n      : 0;\n\n  return [\n    {\n      title: '売上高',\n      value: formatNumber(totalSales),\n      subValue: `目標達成率 ${targetAchievementRate}%`,\n      changeRate: salesChangeRate,\n      isPositive: salesChangeRate >= 0,\n      unit: '円',\n      type: 'sales',\n    },\n    {\n      title: '客数',\n      value: formatNumber(totalCustomers),\n      subValue: `月平均 ${formatNumber(avgMonthlyCustomers)}人`,\n      changeRate: customersChangeRate,\n      isPositive:
